GBA lever session on the board, 2026-10-10. Fork gba-cpu b3ae1d3b, xtensa-68000-dynarec gba-cpu 0506b6a, GBAPROF=1,
gbsp only, 789 104 B (as expected), flashed once; each run sets /sd/retro-go/config/gbaopt.txt from the launcher.
Every run: Mario Kart Super Circuit, "load 1" ~0.6 s after "Retro-Go ready", A held, logger 60 s, unfiltered log.
Values: medians of the LAST 6 GBAPROF / GBAPERF / GBAL1 lines (ranges in brackets). Volume 0.

run  options                      cpu ms/frame           fps   kcycles  I-stall kcyc  cache-miss  L1 miss
1    perf=1                       19.30 (19.11-19.55)    51.5  4660     2487          0 (*)       15.3%
2    perf=1 core1_idle=1          13.72 (13.58-13.79)    60    3285     1527          0 (*)       15.35%
3    perf=1 l1=1024               19.23 (19.03-19.56)    52    4656     2488          0 (*)       10.9%
4    perf=1 l1=2048               19.16 (19.01-19.48)    52    4653     2491          0 (*)        4.6%
5    perf=1 isync=1               19.16 (19.03-19.49)    52    4618     2431          0 (*)       15.25%
6    perf=1 l1=2048 isync=1       19.15 (18.93-19.26)    52    4598     2454          0 (*)        4.6%
7    file deleted (play check)    19.27 (19.12-19.56)    51.5  -        -             -           15.3%
(*) the "cache-miss" field of GBAPERF reads 0 in every line of every run (the counter does not count on this
    chip/config); "busy/PIF" is ~2.36-2.46 Mcycles, close to the whole I-stall.
Every run's log has the "gbaopt from /sd/retro-go/config/gbaopt.txt: perf .., core1_idle .., l1 .., isync .." line
matching the file (run 7: no gbaopt line, file deleted).

Reading:
- I-stall is 2.49 Mcycles of 4.66 per frame (53% of core 0, ~10.4 ms at 240 MHz) in the baseline.
  That is far above the 700 kcycles threshold: locality / instruction fetch is THE lever.
- Run 2 is the headline: with core 1 idle (picture frozen on purpose), I-stall falls by 960 kcycles a frame
  and cpu by 5.6 ms to 13.7 ms = 60 fps. Core 1's renderer competes with core 0 for the shared cache/PSRAM
  path; about 40% of core 0's instruction stall comes from that contention.
- l1=2048 cuts block-lookup misses 15.3% -> 4.6% but gains only ~0.14 ms; isync ~0.14 ms; both together
  (run 6) 19.15 ms = ~0.15 ms banked, below the 0.4-0.5 ms expected.
Run 7 play check (opt build, no file): race played at full speed (webcam), START opens the pause menu
(CONTINUE / TRY AGAIN / QUIT), A continues, A + RIGHT steers; SELECT shows no visible effect in Mario Kart.
